intdb {
	enum LC_MUX_DO {
		DI,
		F5O,
		CO,
	}

	enum FF_MODE {
		FF,
		LATCH,
	}

	enum LC_MUX_D {
		F,
		DO,
	}

	enum IO_SLEW {
		FAST,
		SLOW,
	}

	enum IO_PULL {
		NONE,
		PULLUP,
		PULLDOWN,
	}

	enum SCANTEST_OUT {
		XI,
		YI,
		ZI,
		VI,
		SCANPASS,
	}

	enum RDBK_MUX_CLK {
		CCLK,
		RDBK,
	}

	enum SCAN_TEST {
		DISABLE,
		ENABLE,
		ENLL,
		NE7,
	}

		SLOW,
		MED,
		FAST,
	}

		Q3,

	bel_class LC {
		input F1;
		input F2;
		input F3;
		input F4;
		input DI;
		input CE;
		input CK;
		input CLR;
		output X;
		output Q;
		output DO;
		attribute LUT: bitvec[16];
		attribute MUX_DO: LC_MUX_DO;
		attribute FF_MODE: FF_MODE;
		attribute MUX_D: LC_MUX_D;
		attribute CLR_ENABLE: bool;
		attribute CE_ENABLE: bool;
		attribute READBACK: bitvec[1];
	}

	bel_class TBUF {
		input I;
		input T;
		output O;
		attribute T_ENABLE: bool;
	}

	bel_class PROGTIE {
		output O;
		attribute VAL: bitvec[1];
	}

	bel_class IO {
		input O;
		input T;
		output I;
		pad PAD: inout
		attribute SLEW: IO_SLEW;
		attribute PULL: IO_PULL;
		attribute DELAY_ENABLE: bool;
		attribute INV_I: bool;
	}

	}

	}

		output OUT;

		tile_class CLB {
			cell CELL;
			bitrect MAIN: Vertical (rev 12, rev 34);

			switchbox INT {
				mux OMUX[0] @[MAIN[0][1], MAIN[0][0], MAIN[0][4], MAIN[0][2], MAIN[0][3]] {
					OUT_LC_X[0] = 0b01111,
					OUT_LC_X[1] = 0b00111,
					OUT_LC_X[2] = 0b00011,
					OUT_LC_X[3] = 0b01011,
					OUT_LC_Q[0] = 0b11101,
					OUT_LC_Q[1] = 0b10101,
					OUT_LC_Q[2] = 0b10001,
					OUT_LC_Q[3] = 0b11001,
					OUT_LC_DO[0] = 0b11110,
					OUT_LC_DO[1] = 0b10110,
					OUT_LC_DO[2] = 0b10010,
					OUT_LC_DO[3] = 0b11010,

		tile_class CNR_SW {
			cell CELL;
			bitrect MAIN: Vertical (rev 7, rev 28);

			switchbox INT {
				mux IMUX_RDBK_RCLK @[MAIN[2][24], MAIN[2][25], MAIN[2][23]] {
					SINGLE_IO_W_N[0] = 0b011,
					SINGLE_IO_W_N[1] = 0b001,
					SINGLE_IO_W_N[2] = 0b101,
					SINGLE_IO_W_N[3] = 0b111,

					LONG_H[2] = 0b110,
					LONG_H[3] = 0b100,

				mux IMUX_BUFG @[MAIN[5][27], MAIN[5][26], MAIN[6][26], MAIN[6][27], MAIN[2][18], MAIN[2][17], MAIN[2][16], MAIN[2][15], MAIN[2][14]] {

					SINGLE_IO_W_N[2] = 0b110111111,
					SINGLE_IO_W_N[3] = 0b111011111,
					LONG_V[4] = 0b111101111,
					LONG_V[5] = 0b111110111,
					LONG_V[6] = 0b111111011,
					LONG_V[7] = 0b111111101,
					OUT_CLKIOB = 0b111111110,
				}
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[0] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[1][16];
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[1] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[0][16];
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[2] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[1][20];
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[3] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[0][20];
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[4] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[1][23];
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[5] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[0][24];
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[6] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[1][26];
				pass SINGLE_IO_W_N[7] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[0][27];
				pass LONG_H[0] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[0][14];
				pass LONG_H[1] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[1][15];
				pass LONG_H[2] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[0][18];
				pass LONG_H[3] = OUT_RDBK_RIP @!MAIN[1][18];
				pass LONG_H[4] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[0][22];
				pass LONG_H[5] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[1][22];
				pass LONG_H[6] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[0][26];
				pass LONG_H[7] = OUT_RDBK_DATA @!MAIN[1][24];
				bipass LONG_H[0] = LONG_V[0] @!MAIN[1][11];
				bipass LONG_H[1] = LONG_V[1] @!MAIN[1][13];
				bipass LONG_H[2] = LONG_V[2] @!MAIN[1][14];
				bipass LONG_H[3] = LONG_V[3] @!MAIN[0][10];
				bipass LONG_H[4] = LONG_V[4] @!MAIN[0][11];
				bipass LONG_H[5] = LONG_V[5] @!MAIN[1][9];
				bipass LONG_H[6] = LONG_V[6] @!MAIN[0][8];
				bipass LONG_H[7] = LONG_V[7] @!MAIN[1][8];

		tile_class CNR_SE {
			cell CELL;

			switchbox INT {

				pass SINGLE_IO_S_W[4] = OUT_STARTUP_Q2 @!MAIN[5][25];
				pass SINGLE_IO_S_W[5] = OUT_STARTUP_Q2 @!MAIN[6][25];
				pass SINGLE_IO_S_W[6] = OUT_STARTUP_Q1Q4 @!MAIN[5][27];
				pass SINGLE_IO_S_W[7] = OUT_STARTUP_Q1Q4 @!MAIN[6][27];
				pass LONG_V[0] = OUT_STARTUP_DONEIN @!MAIN[5][20];
				pass LONG_V[1] = OUT_STARTUP_DONEIN @!MAIN[6][20];
				pass LONG_V[2] = OUT_STARTUP_Q3 @!MAIN[5][22];
				pass LONG_V[3] = OUT_STARTUP_Q3 @!MAIN[6][22];
				pass LONG_V[4] = OUT_STARTUP_Q2 @!MAIN[5][24];
				pass LONG_V[5] = OUT_STARTUP_Q2 @!MAIN[6][24];
				pass LONG_V[6] = OUT_STARTUP_Q1Q4 @!MAIN[5][26];
				pass LONG_V[7] = OUT_STARTUP_Q1Q4 @!MAIN[6][26];
				bipass LONG_H[0] = LONG_V[0] @!MAIN[3][14];
				bipass LONG_H[1] = LONG_V[1] @!MAIN[3][16];
				bipass LONG_H[2] = LONG_V[2] @!MAIN[3][18];

				attribute GSR_TIMING @[MAIN[0][18], MAIN[0][19]] {

					Q2 = 0b11,
					Q3 = 0b10,
					DONE_IN = 0b00,
				}
				attribute SYNC_TO_DONE @!MAIN[1][17];
				attribute MUX_CLK @[MAIN[0][26]] {

					USERCLK = 0b0,
				}

				attribute OSC2_DIV @[MAIN[4][18], MAIN[5][19], MAIN[5][18]] {

				attribute PROG_PULLUP @MAIN[0][16];

			}

			bitrect MAIN: Vertical (rev 7, rev 28);

			switchbox INT {
				mux IMUX_BSCAN_TDO1 @[MAIN[5][1], MAIN[5][0]] {
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[6] = 0b01,
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[7] = 0b00,
					LONG_V[6] = 0b11,
					LONG_V[7] = 0b10,
				}
				mux IMUX_BSCAN_TDO2 @[MAIN[5][3], MAIN[5][2]] {
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[4] = 0b01,
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[5] = 0b00,
					LONG_V[4] = 0b11,
					LONG_V[5] = 0b10,
				}
				mux IMUX_BUFG @[MAIN[5][11], MAIN[5][12], MAIN[5][13], MAIN[5][15], MAIN[5][5], MAIN[5][6], MAIN[5][7], MAIN[5][8], MAIN[5][4]] {
					TIE_0 = 0b111111111,
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[0] = 0b011111111,
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[1] = 0b101111111,
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[2] = 0b110111111,
					SINGLE_IO_N_E[3] = 0b111011111,
					LONG_H[4] = 0b111101111,
					LONG_H[5] = 0b111110111,
					LONG_H[6] = 0b111111011,
					LONG_H[7] = 0b111111101,
					OUT_CLKIOB = 0b111111110,
				}
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[0] = OUT_BSCAN_DRCK @!MAIN[1][7];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[0] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL2 @!MAIN[0][7];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[1] = OUT_BSCAN_DRCK @!MAIN[0][6];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[1] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL2 @!MAIN[1][6];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[2] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL1 @!MAIN[0][4];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[2] = OUT_BSCAN_SHIFT @!MAIN[1][4];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[3] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL1 @!MAIN[1][3];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[3] = OUT_BSCAN_SHIFT @!MAIN[0][3];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[4] = OUT_BSCAN_UPDATE @!MAIN[1][2];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[5] = OUT_BSCAN_UPDATE @!MAIN[0][2];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[6] = OUT_BSCAN_IDLE @!MAIN[0][1];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[6] = OUT_BSCAN_RESET @!MAIN[1][1];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[7] = OUT_BSCAN_IDLE @!MAIN[1][0];
				pass SINGLE_IO_N_E[7] = OUT_BSCAN_RESET @!MAIN[0][0];
				pass LONG_V[0] = OUT_BSCAN_DRCK @!MAIN[0][8];
				pass LONG_V[0] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL2 @!MAIN[1][8];
				pass LONG_V[1] = OUT_BSCAN_DRCK @!MAIN[1][5];
				pass LONG_V[1] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL2 @!MAIN[0][5];
				pass LONG_V[2] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL1 @!MAIN[2][9];
				pass LONG_V[2] = OUT_BSCAN_SHIFT @!MAIN[2][8];
				pass LONG_V[3] = OUT_BSCAN_SEL1 @!MAIN[2][6];
				pass LONG_V[3] = OUT_BSCAN_SHIFT @!MAIN[2][7];
				pass LONG_V[4] = OUT_BSCAN_UPDATE @!MAIN[2][5];
				pass LONG_V[5] = OUT_BSCAN_UPDATE @!MAIN[2][4];
				pass LONG_V[6] = OUT_BSCAN_IDLE @!MAIN[2][3];
				pass LONG_V[6] = OUT_BSCAN_RESET @!MAIN[2][2];
				pass LONG_V[7] = OUT_BSCAN_IDLE @!MAIN[2][0];
				pass LONG_V[7] = OUT_BSCAN_RESET @!MAIN[2][1];
				bipass LONG_H[0] = LONG_V[0] @!MAIN[3][13];
				bipass LONG_H[1] = LONG_V[1] @!MAIN[3][11];
				bipass LONG_H[2] = LONG_V[2] @!MAIN[3][9];
				bipass LONG_H[3] = LONG_V[3] @!MAIN[3][7];
				bipass LONG_H[4] = LONG_V[4] @!MAIN[3][5];
				bipass LONG_H[5] = LONG_V[5] @!MAIN[3][3];
				bipass LONG_H[6] = LONG_V[6] @!MAIN[3][1];
				bipass LONG_H[7] = LONG_V[7] @!MAIN[3][0];
			}

			switchbox BUFG {
				permabuf GCLK_NW = IMUX_BUFG;
			}

			bel CLKIOB {
				output OUT = OUT_CLKIOB;
			}

			bel BSCAN {
				input TDO1 = IMUX_BSCAN_TDO1;
				input TDO2 = IMUX_BSCAN_TDO2;
				output DRCK = OUT_BSCAN_DRCK;
				output IDLE = OUT_BSCAN_IDLE;
				output RESET = OUT_BSCAN_RESET;
				output SEL1 = OUT_BSCAN_SEL1;
				output SEL2 = OUT_BSCAN_SEL2;
				output SHIFT = OUT_BSCAN_SHIFT;
				output UPDATE = OUT_BSCAN_UPDATE;
				attribute ENABLE @!MAIN[0][9];
				attribute RECONFIG @!MAIN[5][9];
				attribute READBACK @!MAIN[3][15];
			}

			bel MISC_NW {
				attribute IO_INPUT_MODE @[MAIN[5][10]] {
					TTL = 0b1,
					CMOS = 0b0,
				}
			}

			// wire OUT_CLKIOB                     CLKIOB.OUT
			// wire OUT_BSCAN_DRCK                 BSCAN.DRCK
			// wire OUT_BSCAN_IDLE                 BSCAN.IDLE
			// wire OUT_BSCAN_RESET                BSCAN.RESET
			// wire OUT_BSCAN_SEL1                 BSCAN.SEL1
			// wire OUT_BSCAN_SEL2                 BSCAN.SEL2
			// wire OUT_BSCAN_SHIFT                BSCAN.SHIFT
			// wire OUT_BSCAN_UPDATE               BSCAN.UPDATE
			// wire IMUX_BSCAN_TDO1                BSCAN.TDO1
			// wire IMUX_BSCAN_TDO2                BSCAN.TDO2
		}

		tile_class CNR_NE {
			cell CELL;
			bitrect MAIN: Vertical (rev 8, rev 28);

			switchbox INT {
				mux IMUX_OSC_OCLK @[MAIN[6][5], MAIN[6][4], MAIN[6][3], MAIN[6][2], MAIN[6][6]] {
					TIE_0 = 0b11111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[0] = 0b01111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[1] = 0b10111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[2] = 0b11011,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[3] = 0b11101,
					LONG_H[0] = 0b01110,
					LONG_H[1] = 0b10110,
					LONG_H[2] = 0b11010,
					LONG_H[3] = 0b11100,
				}
				mux IMUX_BYPOSC_PUMP @[MAIN[5][4], MAIN[5][5], MAIN[5][2], MAIN[5][3]] {
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[4] = 0b0111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[5] = 0b1011,
					LONG_H[4] = 0b1101,
					LONG_V[3] = 0b1110,
					off = 0b1111,
				}
				mux IMUX_BUFG @[MAIN[3][10], MAIN[3][11], MAIN[1][11], MAIN[1][10], MAIN[3][3], MAIN[3][2], MAIN[3][1], MAIN[3][0], MAIN[3][4]] {
					TIE_0 = 0b111111111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[0] = 0b011111111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[1] = 0b101111111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[2] = 0b110111111,
					SINGLE_IO_E_S[3] = 0b111011111,
					LONG_V[4] = 0b111101111,
					LONG_V[5] = 0b111110111,
					LONG_V[6] = 0b111111011,
					LONG_V[7] = 0b111111101,
					OUT_CLKIOB = 0b111111110,
				}
				pass SINGLE_IO_E_S[2] = OUT_BSUPD @!MAIN[4][7];
				pass SINGLE_IO_E_S[3] = OUT_BSUPD @!MAIN[4][6];
				pass SINGLE_IO_E_S[4] = OUT_OSC_OSC1 @!MAIN[6][1];
				pass SINGLE_IO_E_S[5] = OUT_OSC_OSC1 @!MAIN[5][1];
				pass SINGLE_IO_E_S[6] = OUT_OSC_OSC2 @!MAIN[5][0];
				pass SINGLE_IO_E_S[7] = OUT_OSC_OSC2 @!MAIN[6][0];
				pass LONG_H[2] = OUT_BSUPD @!MAIN[4][5];
				pass LONG_H[3] = OUT_BSUPD @!MAIN[4][4];
				pass LONG_H[4] = OUT_OSC_OSC1 @!MAIN[4][3];
				pass LONG_H[5] = OUT_OSC_OSC1 @!MAIN[4][2];
				pass LONG_H[6] = OUT_OSC_OSC2 @!MAIN[4][1];
				pass LONG_H[7] = OUT_OSC_OSC2 @!MAIN[4][0];
				bipass LONG_H[0] = LONG_V[0] @!MAIN[1][7];
				bipass LONG_H[1] = LONG_V[1] @!MAIN[1][6];
				bipass LONG_H[2] = LONG_V[2] @!MAIN[1][5];
				bipass LONG_H[3] = LONG_V[3] @!MAIN[1][4];
				bipass LONG_H[4] = LONG_V[4] @!MAIN[1][3];
				bipass LONG_H[5] = LONG_V[5] @!MAIN[1][2];
				bipass LONG_H[6] = LONG_V[6] @!MAIN[1][1];
				bipass LONG_H[7] = LONG_V[7] @!MAIN[1][0];
			}

			switchbox BUFG {
				permabuf GCLK_NE = IMUX_BUFG;
			}

			bel CLKIOB {
				output OUT = OUT_CLKIOB;
			}

			bel OSC_NE {
				input C = IMUX_OSC_OCLK;
				output OSC1 = OUT_OSC_OSC1;
				output OSC2 = OUT_OSC_OSC2;
			}

			bel BYPOSC {
				input I = IMUX_BYPOSC_PUMP;
			}

			bel BSUPD {
				output O = OUT_BSUPD;
			}

			bel MISC_NE {
				attribute TAC @!MAIN[0][10];
				attribute TLC @!MAIN[0][11];
			}

			// wire OUT_CLKIOB                     CLKIOB.OUT
			// wire OUT_BSUPD                      BSUPD.O
			// wire OUT_OSC_OSC1                   OSC_NE.OSC1
			// wire OUT_OSC_OSC2                   OSC_NE.OSC2
			// wire IMUX_OSC_OCLK                  OSC_NE.C
			// wire IMUX_BYPOSC_PUMP               BYPOSC.I
		}
	}

		connector_class CNR_NW {
